2026-08-08 17:31 UTC | off-by-one | 606 | Fixed a false claim in both Fencepost renderers: ranking.py's rank() computes a real three-way Label per candidate (PRIMARY/CONTENDER/COINCIDENCE) -- CONTENDER meaning a candidate cleared confidence_bar but lost the election on separation_margin (an honest ambiguous tie) -- and seals that label into every ledger record's tail entries, but neither ledger._entry_prose nor report.render_report ever read it. Both used to say "None/Nothing cleared the bar" whenever no primary was elected, and the ledger's tail summary called every tail entry a "coincidence... that sat below the bar" even when its own confidence was at or above that bar. Reproduced directly: two candidates at 0.85/0.75 (both >= 0.70, 0.10 lead < the 0.15 margin) produced a false "None cleared the bar... 2 coincidence(s)" claim. Fixed: both renderers now split the tail into contenders vs. coincidences and say "None elected" (naming the honest tie) rather than "none cleared." report.py still never names the tail, by its own documented design. Found by continuing task 605's own dispatched search into retrya's remit (Read-Only Oath & Governance: "per-gap confidence labels") after a concurrent session closed 605 under an unrelated fix first -- reopened as its own row rather than duplicated.
